```verilog
    next_pc = pc_ex + imm_ex;  // Branch target
end
```

pc_ex + imm_ex = PC + offset → Branch target.

ALU path alternatif?
- ALU'da PC + imm hesapla? YES!
- alu_a_sel=ALU_A_PC, alu_op=ADD, alu_in2=imm
- Ama basit tasarımlarda separate adder kullanılır.

Sizin tasarım: pc_ex taşıyor (flexible, external adder veya ALU ile hesap).

9. OPERAND DATA FORWARDING

rs1_data_ex, rs2_data_ex ID stage'de register file'dan okundu. Ama forwarding unit tarafından override edilebilir.

ALU input MUX (conceptual):

```verilog
always @(*) begin
    case (forward_a)
        2'b00: alu_in_a = rs1_data_ex;         // Register file
        2'b01: alu_in_a = mem_wb_alu_result;   // MEM/WB stage result
        2'b10: alu_in_a = ex_mem_alu_result;   // EX/MEM stage result
        default: alu_in_a = 32'bx;
    endcase
    
    case (forward_b)
        2'b00: alu_in_b = (alu_src_ex) ? imm_ex : rs2_data_ex;
        2'b01: alu_in_b = mem_wb_alu_result;
        2'b10: alu_in_b = ex_mem_alu_result;
        default: alu_in_b = 32'bx;
    endcase
end
```

ID/EX register'dan rs1_data_ex, rs2_data_ex 32-bit data buses olarak ALU'ya bağlanır. Forwarding override edebilir.

10. DATA DEPENDENCY HANDLING

Örnek: Register dependency

Instruction 1: ADD x1, x2, x3      (Cycle 3: EX stage)
Instruction 2: AND x4, x1, x5      (Cycle 3: ID stage)

Cycle 3:
  EX: ADD x1
      - rs1_ex=2, rs2_ex=3
      - alu_op_ex=ADD
      - alu_result calc: x2 + x3
      
  ID: AND x4, x1, x5
      - ID/EX register: rs1_id=1 (x1), rs2_id=5
      - rs1_data_id = x1'nin value (stale! Not updated yet)
      
  Forwarding Unit:
      - ex_mem_rd != 1 (Not in MEM stage yet)
      - mem_wb_rd != 1 (No previous write)
      - forward_a = 00 (no forwarding, use register file data)
      
  PROBLEM: x1 stale value!

Cycle 4:
  EX: AND x4 (using stale x1)
      - rs1_ex=1 (x1), rs2_ex=5
      - rs1_data_ex stale
      
  MEM: ADD x1 (alu_result_mem ready)
  
  Forwarding Unit:
      - ex_mem_rd=1 (AND's destination X wait, EX/MEM stage ADD's rd=1!)
      - ex_mem_reg_write_en=1
      - forward_a = 10 (Forward from EX/MEM)
      
  alu_in_a = ex_mem_alu_result (ADD's result) ✓
  Correct!

Key: Pipeline forwarding architecture saves register file read latency. ID/EX rs1_data_ex is initial value, forwarding override.

11. TIMING ANALIZ

ID/EX register FF bank:

32 registers * 29 signals = ~928 flip-flops total (1,728 transistors in CMOS)

Critical paths:

Path 1 (data): 
- ID stage combinational: ~5 ns
- ID/EX FF: tco (clock-to-output) = ~3 ns
- EX stage setup: ~2 ns
- Total: 10 ns (good for 100 MHz)

Path 2 (forwarding):
- ID/EX FF: tco = 3 ns
- Forwarding Unit: ~2 ns (combinational)
- MUX selector: ~1 ns
- Total: 6 ns (very fast)

Path 3 (hazard detection):
- ID/EX FF: tco = 3 ns
- Hazard detection logic: ~3 ns
- Stall signal: ~1 ns
- Total: 7 ns (fast, no critical)

Timing closure: All paths < 10 ns (good for 100+ MHz).

12. PROTOTIP-1 vs PROTOTIP-2 (SIZIN)

Sizin comment:

// Degisiklik: funct3 eklenmisti (Prototip-1'de de vardi), ayni kalir.

Anlamı: funct3 Prototip-1'de zaten vardı, Prototip-2'de aynı.

funct3 nedir?
- Load/Store width: LB=0, LH=1, LW=2, LBU=4, LHU=5
- Branch type: BEQ=0, BNE=1, BLT=4, BGE=5, BLTU=6, BGEU=7
- Shift type: SLL=1, SRL=5, SRA=5 (with funct7[5])

funct3_ex taşınması önemli (byte-enable logic MEM stage'de).

Sizin tasarım consistent (Prototip 1 ve 2 aynı).

13. GITHUB POPÜLERİTESİ TASARIMLARIYLA KARŞILAŞTIRMA

PicoRV32 (Clifford Wolf) - Minimal Pipeline
GitHub: https://github.com/cliffordwolf/picorv32
Stars: 4.1k

PicoRV32 ID/EX register:
- Simpler structure
- No separate stall/flush logic
- Register freeze implicit
- ~100 lines

Sizinle Fark: Sizin tasarım explicit stall/flush handling (better clarity).

VexRISCV (SpinalHDL) - Parametric
GitHub: https://github.com/SpinalHDL/VexRISCV
Stars: 1.4k

VexRISCV ID/EX register:
- Pluggable pipeline depth
- Parameterized signal widths
- Hazard detection integrated
- Scala-based (not Verilog)

Sizinle Fark: Scala vs Verilog, parameterization level.

Rocket Chip (UC Berkeley)
GitHub: https://github.com/chipsalliance/rocket-chip
Stars: 1.8k

Rocket Chip ID/EX register:
- Multiple-issue width (3-4 instructions)
- Complex register matrix
- Exception handling
- ~500 lines per pipeline stage

Sizinle Fark: Enterprise complexity.

CVA6 (Thales) - Enterprise
GitHub: https://github.com/openhwgroup/cva6
Stars: 1.2k

CVA6 ID/EX register:
- Out-of-order pipeline
- Register renaming
- Scoreboard
- Load/Store queue support

14. HAZARD HANDLING INTEGRATION

ID/EX register ve Hazard Detection Unit tight integration:

```verilog
// In main CPU
hazard_detection_unit hdu (
    .if_id_rs1(if_id_rs1),
    .if_id_rs2(if_id_rs2),
    .if_id_uses_rs1(if_id_uses_rs1),
    .if_id_uses_rs2(if_id_uses_rs2),
    .id_ex_rd(id_ex_rd),           // From ID/EX register
    .id_ex_mem_read(id_ex_dmem_we), // From ID/EX register
    .stall(stall),
    .pc_write_en(pc_write_en),
    .if_id_write_en(if_id_write_en),
    .id_ex_flush(id_ex_flush)
);

// ID/EX register control
id_ex_reg id_ex (
    .clk(clk),
    .rst_n(rst_n),
    .flush(id_ex_flush),           // From HDU
    .stall(stall),                 // From HDU
    .pc_plus_4_id(pc_plus_4_id),
    ...
);
```

id_ex_rd ve id_ex_mem_read sinyalleri hazard detection için next cycle read'leniyor.

Timing: HDU' output (flush, stall) → FF input (setup time ~2-3 ns satisfied).

15. BYPASS PATH OPTIMIZATION

Bazı instruction'larda rs1_data_ex veya rs2_data_ex immediate override edilebilir:

```verilog
// Conceptual
alu_src_ex control:
if (alu_src_ex == 1) begin
    alu_in_b = imm_ex;  // I-type: use immediate
end else begin
    alu_in_b = rs2_data_ex;  // R-type: use rs2
end
```

alu_src_ex ID/EX register'dan geliyor, Control Unit tarafından set edildi.

Değişiklik yok Prototip-2'de ama important integrated feature.

16. CRITICAL CONTROL SIGNALS

rd_ex:  Register destination (5-bit)

EX/MEM, MEM/WB ve WB stage'ine pass'lanıyor. Forwarding unit tarafından checked.

alu_op_ex:  ALU operation (4-bit)

EX stage ALU'ya istediğimiz operation belirt.

reg_write_en_ex:  Register write enable (1-bit)

WB stage'de register file write izni. Load, arithmetic: 1, Store: 0.

dmem_we_ex:  Data memory write enable (1-bit)

Store instructions için 1, diğerleri 0.

mem_to_reg_ex:  Memory-to-register (1-bit)

Load instructions: Register'a memory data yaz (not ALU result).

17. STATE MACHINE (IMPLICIT)

ID/EX register'da state management:

State 1: RESET
  All outputs = 0

State 2: NORMAL
  (flush=0, stall=0) → Update all

State 3: STALL
  (stall=1) → Hold (no update)

State 4: FLUSH
  (flush=1) → Control signals = 0

Transition:

RESET ----(posedge clk, rst_n=1)---> NORMAL
NORMAL ---(if flush=1, hazard)---> FLUSH
NORMAL ---(if stall=1, load)---> STALL
STALL -----(hazard resolved)-----> NORMAL
FLUSH -----(next cycle)-----------> NORMAL

No explicit FSM, implicit control flow (combinational always block).

18. SİZİN TASARIMIN GÜÇLÜ YÖNLERI

1. Explicit stall ve flush handling (clarity)
2. Tüm pipeline operand ve kontrol sinyallerini taşıyor
3. Correct reset, stall, flush behavior
4. Non-blocking assignment (<=) ile no race condition
5. Parametrik XLEN (generics)
6. Asynchronous reset (startup safety)
7. Clean Verilog syntax

19. EKSİKLİKLER VE İYİLEŞTİRMELER

Eksiklik 1: Multi-cycle operation latency tracking yok

MUL 3-cycle, DIV 5-cycle instruction'lar varsa:

```verilog
input wire [2:0] id_ex_result_latency;  // 0=1cycle, 3=mul, 5=div
output reg [2:0] id_ex_result_latency_ex;

// Hazard detection: Stall (latency-1) cycles
```

Eksiklik 2: Exception/interrupt fields yok

Illegal instruction, page fault, etc.:

```verilog
input wire [31:0] id_ex_exception_code;
output reg [31:0] id_ex_exception_code_ex;
```

Eksiklik 3: Virtual memory (TLB) support yok

Simulated address → Physical address mapping:

```verilog
input wire [31:0] id_ex_virt_addr;
output reg [31:0] id_ex_virt_addr_ex;
```

Eksiklik 4: Performance monitoring yok

Instruction type tracking:

```verilog
output reg id_ex_is_load;
output reg id_ex_is_store;
output reg id_ex_is_branch;
```

20. SONUÇ

Sizin id_ex_reg.v tasarımı:

Güçlü Tarafları:
- Complete operand ve control signal transfer
- Correct flush/stall handling
- Forwarding unit support (rs1, rs2 register addresses)
- Hazard detection integration
- Clean reset state
- Efficient timing (< 10 ns path)

Eksikleri:
- Multi-cycle operation support yok
- Exception handling yok
- Virtual memory yok
- Performance counters yok

RV32I 5-stage pipeline için IDEAL! Advanced features için extended yapılabilir.

Register count: ~29 signals, ~928 flip-flops
Transistor count: ~1,728 (CMOS technology)
Power: ~5-10 mW per 100 MHz (static + dynamic)
Timing: Critical path ~10 ns (suitable for 100+ MHz clocks)

================================================================================
IF_ID_REG.V ANALİZİ (IF/ID Pipeline Register)
=============

1. YAPININ GENEL ÖZETİ

Sizin IF/ID pipeline register modülü, instruction fetch (IF) aşamasından instruction decode (ID) aşamasına ham instruction ve program counter bilgisini taşıyan en basit pipeline register'ıdır.

Temel işlev:
- Instruction memory'den fetch edilen 32-bit instruction'ı sakla
- PC (program counter) ve PC+4 bilgisini taşı
- IF stage'de fetch edilen instruction'ın branch prediction bilgisini dekode aşamasına ilet
- Stall sinyaline göre register freeze et (load hazard sırasında)
- Flush sinyaline göre NOP instruction inject et (branch misprediction sonrası)

Veri akışı: Instruction Memory → IF/ID register → ID stage (Control Unit, Register File)

Kritik nokta: EX stage'de branch resolveuldiyinde IF/ID register'a flush sinyali gelir, bu register'da NOP inject edilir, böylece speculative instruction'lar pipeline'dan temizlenir.

2. MODULE PARAMETRESI

parameter XLEN = 32

Standart 32-bit mimarı. RV64 için XLEN=64. Instruction hep 32-bit (compressed RV32C desteğiyse 16-bit alternatif var ama sizin tasarımda 32-bit fixed).

3. GİRİŞ SİNYALLERİ (IF aşamasından)

pc_plus_4_if:       32-bit (XLEN bit)
Bir sonraki instruction'un adresi (PC + 4)
Neden önemli? Sequential execution için. JAL/JALR sonra x1 (link register)'a yazılacak return address.
Örnek: Instruction adres=0x1000, pc_plus_4_if = 0x1004

pc_if:              32-bit (XLEN bit)
Bu instruction'ın actual program counter adres
Neden önemli? Branch target calculation (PC-relative jumps): target = PC + offset
Örnek: BEQ at 0x1000 offset=8 → target = 0x1000 + 8 = 0x1008

instruction_if:     32-bit (fixed, not XLEN!)
Ham fetch edilen instruction, 32 bit sabit
Yapı:
- Bits 6:0 = opcode
- Bits 14:12 = funct3
- Bits 31:25 = funct7
- Bits 19:15 = rs1
- Bits 24:20 = rs2
- Bits 11:7 = rd
- Bits 31:20 = immediate (I-type)
- Etc. (depending on instruction type)

Örnek: LW x1, 0(x2) = 0x00210083
- opcode = 7'b0000011 (LOAD)
- rd = 5'b00001 (x1)
- funct3 = 3'b010 (word load)
- rs1 = 5'b00010 (x2)
- imm = 12'b000000000000 (0)

4. ÇIKIŞ SİNYALLERİ (ID aşamasına)

Çıkışlar giriş sinyalleriyle tamamen aynı, sadece "_id" suffiksi:

pc_plus_4_id:   32-bit
instruction_id: 32-bit
pc_id:          32-bit

Bu sinyaller ID stage'deki bileşenlere dağıtılır:
- Control Unit: instruction_id'yi opcode, funct3, funct7 alanlarına parça
- Register File: rs1_id, rs2_id alanlarını register address olarak kullan
- Immediate Generator: instruction_id'den imm fields çıkar
- Hazard Detection: instruction_id'den opcode türünü (uses_rs1/uses_rs2) determine et
- PC adder: pc_id + offset → branch target

5. CONTROL SİNYALLERİ

flush:      1-bit
=1 → Branch misprediction veya exception, pipeline flush, NOP inject
=0 → Normal operation

flush nasıl trigger olur?
- Branch resolved (EX stage) yanlış tahminde
- Exception detected
- Interrupt incoming

Timing: EX stage'de branch condition hesap (Cycle N), flush signal Cycle N+1'de if_id_reg'a gelir.

stall:      1-bit
=1 → Load-use hazard detected, register freeze
=0 → Normal operation

stall nasıl trigger olur?
- Hazard Detection Unit detects load-use
- pc_write_en=0, if_id_write_en=0 (PC ve IF/ID frozen)

6. SEQUENTIAL LOGIC - DETAILED

always @(posedge clk or negedge rst_n) begin
    if (!rst_n) begin
        // System reset
        pc_plus_4_id  <= {XLEN{1'b0}};
        instruction_id <= 32'h00000013;  // NOP
        pc_id          <= {XLEN{1'b0}};
    end else if (flush || stall) begin
        // Flush or Stall
        if (flush) begin
            pc_plus_4_id  <= {XLEN{1'b0}};
            instruction_id <= 32'h00000013;  // NOP
            pc_id          <= {XLEN{1'b0}};
        end
        // If stall only, keep values (implicit - no update)
    end else begin
        // Normal: pass through
        pc_plus_4_id  <= pc_plus_4_if;
        instruction_id <= instruction_if;
        pc_id          <= pc_if;
    end
end

REVİYE: 3 state, 2 control signal (flush, stall)

E1. RESET STATE (rst_n=0)

System startup veya reset button:

pc_plus_4_id  = 0x00000000
instruction_id = 0x00000013 (ADDI x0, x0, 0 - NOP)
pc_id = 0x00000000

Neden NOP?
- 0x00000013 = ADDI x0, x0, imm=0
- opcode = 7'b0010011 (ADDI)
- rd = 5'b00000 (x0)
- rs1 = 5'b00000 (x0)
- imm = 12'b000000000000 (0)
- Effect: x0 = x0 + 0 = 0 (no change, since x0 is hardwired)

Kontrolü açısından:
- Control Unit: ADDI decode → alu_op=ADD, reg_write_en=1
- Register File: x0'ya yazma atlanır (hardwired)
- ALU: 0 + 0 = 0
- Result: Harmless, no side effect

SAFER: All 0 opcodes (0x00000000) decode'lenirse undefined instruction. Specification dan NOP tercih edilmiş (good design choice).

E2. FLUSH STATE (flush=1 OR stall=1)

İki case:

Case A: flush=1 (Branch misprediction)

if (flush) begin
    pc_plus_4_id  <= {XLEN{1'b0}};
    instruction_id <= 32'h00000013;  // NOP
    pc_id          <= {XLEN{1'b0}};
end

Instruction register'ı NOP ile override edilir. Kontrol açısından:
- ID stage'de NOP decode edilir
- Control signals: normal op signals (reg_write_en=1, for example)
- Ama NOP'tur, side effect yok
- Next cycle'da normal instruction fetch devam eder

Timing:
Cycle N: Branch resolved in EX, branch_condition decide
Cycle N+1: flush signal IF/ID'ye girdi, NOP inject edildi
Cycle N+2: Normal instruction resume

Case B: stall=1, flush=0 (Load hazard)

else if (stall == 1) begin
    // Don't update (implicit - no assignment)
end

Register'lar frozen kalır. IF/ID register'da aynı instruction value sabit:

pc_plus_4_id: (unchanged)
instruction_id: (unchanged)
pc_id: (unchanged)

Effect: ID stage'de aynı instruction yine decode edilmez (ID/EX frozen too via if_id_write_en=0).

Eger stall state açılırsa (hazard resolved):

Cycle N: hazard detected, stall=1
Cycle N+1: instruction data hold devam, IF stalled (PC frozen)
Cycle N+2: memory data ready, forwarding able, stall=0
Cycle N+3: Normal resume, new instruction fetch start

E3. NORMAL STATE (flush=0, stall=0)

Normal pipeline operation:

pc_plus_4_id  <= pc_plus_4_if;
instruction_id <= instruction_if;
pc_id          <= pc_if;

IF stage çıkış doğrudan IF/ID register'a yazılır. ID stage sıradaki cycle'da bu bilgiyi okur.

Timing (rising clock edge):

Before clock edge:
- IF stage: Instruction memory output stable
- pc_plus_4_if, instruction_if, pc_if bus ready

At clock edge:
- Non-blocking assignment: New values FF'ler tarafından accepted

After clock edge:
- IF/ID register: New values stable
- ID stage: Can read IF/ID register outputs

Pipelined operasyon: İkinci instruction IF'de, birinci instruction aynı anda ID'de.

7. NOP İNSTRUCTİON ENCODING

32'h00000013 = 0b00000000000000000000000000010011

Breakdown:
- Bits [31:20] = 0x000 (immediate = 0)
- Bits [19:15] = 0x00 (rs1 = 0)
- Bits [14:12] = 0x0 (funct3 = 0)
- Bits [11:7] = 0x00 (rd = 0)
- Bits [6:0] = 0x13 (opcode = ADDI)

Result:
ADDI x0, x0, 0 → x0 register'a 0+0 yazılır
x0 hardwired → no actual write
Side effect free!

Alternativ NOP'lar:

Option 1: ADD x0, x0, x0
= 0x00000033 (opcode=0x33, not 0x00000013)
- Similar effect

Option 2: All zeros
= 0x00000000
- Undefined instruction (per RISC-V spec)
- Some CPU's may fault
- Sizin 0x00000013 safer

Choice 0x00000013: RISC-V standard NOP (legal, no exception).

8. FLUSH vs STALL CONDITIONAL LOGİC

Verilog:

if (!rst_n) begin
    // Reset
end else if (flush || stall) begin
    if (flush) begin
        // Flush: inject NOP
    end
    // Else stall: keep (no update)
end else begin
    // Normal
end

Logic evaluation:

Condition 1: (!rst_n) → Reset
- Priority highest (async reset always wins)

Condition 2: (flush || stall)
- If either signal high, enter this block
- Sub-condition: if (flush) → NOP
- Sub-condition: else (stall only) → Hold

Condition 3: else
- Normal operation

State transitions:

NORMAL → (flush=1 or hazard detected) → FLUSH/STALL
FLUSH → (next cycle, flush released) → NORMAL
STALL → (memory data ready, stall released) → NORMAL

Eger flush=1 ve stall=1 aynı anda (unusual):

if (flush || stall) → TRUE
if (flush) → TRUE
→ NOP inject

stall ignored (flush takes priority). Correct: Flush more serious (branch misprediction).

9. CONTROL SINYALLERI KAYNAĞI

flush: EX stage'den gelir
- Branch resolved, condition false → flush=1
- Or mispredict, need recover → flush=1
- PC yeni target'a jump, IF fetch başın yeniden starta

stall, if_id_write_en: Hazard Detection Unit'den gelir
- Load-use hazard detected → stall=1, if_id_write_en=0
- PC freeze, IF/ID freeze

Integration (CPU top level):

```verilog
// Branch resolution in EX stage
assign flush_if_id = (branch_resolved && !branch_condition);

// Hazard detection
hazard_detection_unit hdu (
    ...
    .stall(stall),
    .if_id_write_en(if_id_write_en),
);

// IF/ID register
if_id_reg if_id (
    .clk(clk),
    .rst_n(rst_n),
    .flush(flush_if_id),
    .stall(stall),
    ...
);
```

10. PC KAYNAĞININ GEOMETRİSİ

PC generation (IF stage):

```verilog
// Sequential: normal instruction progression
pc_next_normal = pc + 4;

// Branch: jump to target
pc_next_branch = pc + offset;  // or pc_base + imm for JALR

// Multiplexed:
pc_next = (branch_taken) ? pc_next_branch : pc_next_normal;
```

IF/ID register holds (pc, pc+4) from current cycle.
EX stage branch resolution tetikler flush if mispredicted.

Timeline:

Cycle 1:
  IF: Branch instruction fetch
  PC = 0x1000

Cycle 2:
  IF: Next instruction fetch @ 0x1004 (speculative)
  ID: Branch instruction (assume branch taken)
  
  Problem: Yanlış tahminde!
  
Cycle 3:
  IF: Wrong instruction @ 0x1008 (speculative)
  ID: Next (wrong speculative)
  EX: Branch condition calculated (false!)
  
  Branch resolves false:
  - Flush IF/ID (NOP inject)
  - Jump PC to 0x1004 (fallthrough address)
  - IF: Correct instruction fetch @ 0x1004
  
Cycle 4:
  IF: @ 0x1008 (correct)
  ID: @ 0x1004 (correct, from flushed and recovered)
  EX: (NOP bubble from flush)

10 11. INSTRUCTION DECODE INTEGRATION

IF/ID register instruction_id output → Control Unit:

```verilog
rv32_control control_unit (
    .instruction(instruction_id),
    .opcode(instruction_id[6:0]),
    .funct3(instruction_id[14:12]),
    .funct7(instruction_id[31:25]),
    .rd(instruction_id[11:7]),
    .rs1(instruction_id[19:15]),
    .rs2(instruction_id[24:20]),
    ...
);
```

Control Unit: Combinational logic
- Decode instruction_id fields
- Generate alu_op, alu_src, reg_write_en, etc.
- Pass to ID/EX register next cycle

Register File:

```verilog
regfile #(32, 32) rf (
    .addr_a(instruction_id[19:15]),  // rs1
    .addr_b(instruction_id[24:20]),  // rs2
    .dout_a(rs1_data),
    .dout_b(rs2_data),
    ...
);
```

Register File: Combinational read
- Rs1, rs2 address immediate use
- Data available same cycle (ID stage)

12. TIMING ANALIZ

IF/ID register FF bank:

- 3 registers: pc_plus_4_id, instruction_id, pc_id
- Total: 32 + 32 + 32 = 96-bit bus (96 flip-flops)
- Transistor: ~1.7k (CMOS)

Critical path:

Path 1 (Instruction to Control Unit):
- IF stage combinational: 5 ns (instruction memory)
- IF/ID FF: tco = 3 ns (clock to output)
- Control Unit: combinational = 3 ns (opcode decode)
- Total: 11 ns (CRITICAL for 100 MHz!)

Path 2 (PC to ALU):
- IF stage: 3 ns (PC logic)
- IF/ID FF: tco = 3 ns
- Branch adder: 2 ns
- Total: 8 ns

Path 3 (Register File):
- IF/ID FF: tco = 3 ns (rs1, rs2 address)
- Register File: taa = 4 ns (address to output)
- Total: 7 ns

Timing closure @ 100 MHz (10 ns clock):
- Path 1: 11 ns > 10 ns CRITICAL!
- Path 2: 8 ns < 10 ns OK
- Path 3: 7 ns < 10 ns OK

Problem: Path 1 timing violated!

Solution:
- Add pipeline register after Control Unit (another FF)
- Or reduce instruction memory latency (cache)
- Or increase clock period

Sizin tasarım assumes instruction memory fast (on-chip RAM).

13. GITHUB POPÜLERİTESİ TASARIMLARIYLA KARŞILAŞTIRMA

PicoRV32 (Clifford Wolf)
GitHub: https://github.com/cliffordwolf/picorv32
Stars: 4.1k

PicoRV32 IF/ID:
- Simpler: No separate pc_if, just instruction
- No explicit flush signal (branch handled differently)
- ~80 lines

Sizinle Fark: Sizin tasarım daha modular (pc separation).

VexRISCV (SpinalHDL)
GitHub: https://github.com/SpinalHDL/VexRISCV
Stars: 1.4k

VexRISCV IF/ID:
- Parametric depth (optional, can be removed)
- Prediction data carry (branch history)
- Fetch buffer support
- Scala-based HDL

Sizinle Fark: VexRISCV branch prediction support'ı.

Rocket Chip (UC Berkeley)
GitHub: https://github.com/chipsalliance/rocket-chip
Stars: 1.8k

Rocket Chip IF/ID:
- 64-bit arch support
- Instruction cache integration
- Miss handling
- Compressed instruction (RV32C/RV64C) decompression
- BTB (Branch Target Buffer) data carry

CVA6 (Thales)
GitHub: https://github.com/openhwgroup/cva6
Stars: 1.2k

CVA6 IF/ID:
- Fetch buffer (multiple instructions)
- Exception data carry
- Virtual address tracking
- Prediction correctness validation

BOOM (OOO CPU)
GitHub: https://github.com/riscv-boom/riscv-boom
Stars: 0.8k

BOOM IF/ID:
- Fetch bundle (4-6 instructions)
- Prediction mask
- Confidence data
- Speculative state

14. BRANCH PREDICTION VE FLUSH

Modern CPU'lar branch prediction buffer (BPB) veya branch target buffer (BTB) kullanır. Sizin tasarım predict'siz (always fetch next sequential).

Basit prediction (not implemented):

```verilog
// If last 2 bits of PC suggest backward branch
// (loop pattern), predict branch taken
if (pc[X:Y] == LOOP_ADDR) begin
    pc_next = target_cache[pc[X:Y]];  // Guessed target
end
```

Sizin design: Conservative (always sequential, flush if wrong).

Effect: Pipeline flush penalty = 2-3 cycle per misprediction.

Modern superscalar: Even with prediction, ~15-20% misprediction rate → 2-3 cycle penalty per miss.

Sizin design: 100% conservative, no prediction benefit, but simpler.

15. COMPRESSED INSTRUCTION SUPPORT (RV32C) - NOT IMPLEMENTED

RISC-V standard: 16-bit compressed instructions optional (RV32C extension).

Sizin tasarım: 32-bit only.

If RV32C support needed:

```verilog
// Decompressor in IF stage
wire [31:0] instruction_decompressed;
assign instruction_decompressed = (compressed) ? decompress(instruction_if[15:0]) : instruction_if;

if_id_reg if_id (
    ...
    .instruction_if(instruction_decompressed),
    ...
);
```

RV32C instruction: 16-bit compress format
Example: C.ADD x1, x2
Length: 2 bytes (vs. 4 bytes for 32-bit ADD)

Benefit: Code size reduction (20-30% smaller binaries).
Cost: Extra decompressor logic (pre-IF stage).

Sizin tasarım skip (RV32I only, full 32-bit).

16. EXCEPTION VE INTERRUPT HANDLING

Sizin IF/ID register: No exception field!

If fault occurs in IF (illegal instruction or page fault):
- Exception code not carried forward
- Next stage can't know about fault

İyileştirme:

```verilog
input wire [31:0] exception_cause_if;
output reg [31:0] exception_cause_id;

// In always block:
if (flush) begin
    exception_cause_id <= 32'b0;  // Clear on flush
end else if (!stall) begin
    exception_cause_id <= exception_cause_if;
end
```

MEM/WB stage'de exception handler:
- CPU state snapshot (PC, registers)
- Jump to exception handler address
- Save return address for resume

Sizin design doesn't support (RV32I base, no exceptions for basic).

17. SİZİN TASARIMIN GÜÇLÜ YÖNLERI

1. Simple, clean Verilog
2. Correct flush/stall behavior
3. NOP injection safe
4. PC separation (pc_id, pc_plus_4_id)
5. Parametrik XLEN
6. Asynchronous reset
7. Non-blocking assignment (no race condition)

18. EKSİKLİKLER VE İYİLEŞTİRMELER

Eksiklik 1: Branch prediction yok
- Conservative approach (always sequential)
- Can add simple pattern prediction

Eksiklik 2: Compressed instruction support yok
- RV32C not supported
- Can add decompressor before if_id_reg

Eksiklik 3: Exception handling yok
- Can carry exception_cause_id

Eksiklik 4: BTB (Branch Target Buffer) yok
- Can add prediction cache

Eksiklik 5: TLB/Virtual memory not supported
- Can carry virtual address

19. INTEGRATION WITH PC LOGIC

PC generation (top-level CPU):

```verilog
// Combinational
wire [XLEN-1:0] pc_next_normal = pc + 4;
wire [XLEN-1:0] pc_next_branch = pc + branch_offset;

wire [XLEN-1:0] pc_next_jalr = alu_result;  // From ALU (indirect jump)

// Multiplexed
wire [XLEN-1:0] pc_next = 
    (jalr_en_ex) ? pc_next_jalr :
    (branch_taken_ex) ? pc_next_branch :
    pc_next_normal;

// Sequential
always @(posedge clk or negedge rst_n) begin
    if (!rst_n) pc <= 32'h0;
    else if (pc_write_en) pc <= pc_next;
    else pc <= pc;  // Hold
end

// PC+4 calculation
wire [XLEN-1:0] pc_plus_4_if = pc + 4;
```

PC control:

pc_write_en signal from Hazard Detection Unit:
- Normal: pc_write_en=1 (PC update)
- Stall: pc_write_en=0 (PC freeze)

Branch flush:

Cycle N (EX): branch_taken_ex calculated
Cycle N+1: pc_next_branch assigned, pc updated
- IF: Fetch from new pc_next_branch
- IF/ID: flush=1, NOP inject

This coordination critical!

20. PERFORMANCE İMPACT

Pipeline flush penalty:

Scenario: Loop branch

```
LOOP: LW x1, 0(x10)    // Load array element
      ADDI x1, x1, 1   // Increment
      SW x1, 0(x10)    // Store back
      ADDI x10, x10, 4 // Next element
      BNE x1, x2, LOOP // Loop condition
```

Cycle-by-cycle:

Cycle 1-5: Normal pipeline fill
Cycle 6: All stages busy

Cycle 7:
- BNE resolves in EX (usually taken = loop back)
- IF/ID gets flush
- next PC should = LOOP_START (0x1000)
- Instead: IF/ID fetches LOOP_START + 4 (mispredicted)

Cycle 8:
- Speculative bubbles cleared
- IF: Correctly fetches from LOOP_START

Performance penalty:
- 1-cycle stall + 1-cycle recovery = 2-cycle delay
- Modern superscalar: 15-20 cycle OOO repair (much worse!)

Sizin design: Simple penalty, acceptable for in-order pipeline.

21. SONUÇ

Sizin if_id_reg.v tasarımı:

Güçlü Tarafları:
- Clean, simple Verilog
- Correct flush behavior (NOP injection)
- Correct stall behavior (register hold)
- PC separation for branch calculation
- Parametrik XLEN
- Asynchronous reset
- Safe default (NOP instead of undefined)

Eksikleri:
- Branch prediction yok
- Compressed instruction (RV32C) yok
- Exception handling yok
- Virtual memory yok
- BTB yok

RV32I 5-stage in-order pipeline için PERFECT!

Register count: 3 * 32-bit = 96 flip-flops
Transistor: ~1700 (CMOS)
Power: ~2-3 mW @ 100 MHz
Timing: Critical path ~11 ns (borderline for 100 MHz, safe for 80-90 MHz)

Pipeline flush penalty: 2-3 cycles per misprediction (acceptable for in-order)
Prediction disabled: Conservative but simple trade-off

================================================================================
MEM_WB_REG.V ANALİZİ (MEM/WB Pipeline Register - Write-Back Stage)
=============

1. YAPININ GENEL ÖZETİ

Sizin MEM/WB pipeline register modülü, memory access (MEM) aşamasından write-back (WB) aşamasına veriyi taşıyan en son pipeline register'ıdır. 5-aşamalı pipeline'ın son halkası.

Temel işlev:
- MEM stage'den ALU sonucu, memory read data, kontrol sinyallerini sakla
- WB stage'inde register dosyasına yazılacak data hazırla
- Register destination adresini taşı
- Load byte/halfword işlemleri için funct3 taşı (sign-extension kontrolü)

Veri akışı: Memory → MEM/WB register → Register File Write (WB stage) → CPU register

Kritik nokta: Bu register'da tutulan rd_wb ve register value'su forwarding unit tarafından next cycle'da okunu. MEM/WB hazard detection'ın en past aşaması.

2. MODULE PARAMETRESI

parameter XLEN = 32

Standart 32-bit mimarı. RV64 için XLEN=64. Tüm veri sinyalleri [XLEN-1:0] boyutlu.

3. GİRİŞ SİNYALLERİ (MEM aşamasından)

A. Veri Sinyalleri:

pc_plus_4_mem:      32-bit
Bir sonraki instruction adres (PC + 4)
Neden? JAL/JALR instruction'larında x1 (link register)'a yazılacak return address
Örnek: JAL x1, target → PC+4 return address olarak x1'e yazılır
Cycle: MEM → WB → x1 = PC+4

alu_result_mem:     32-bit
ALU çıkış sonucu (ADD, SUB, AND, OR, vs. işlemler için)
Contention: Arithmetic instruction'lar, LOAD address (memory'den okunan değer, not address)
Örnek: ADD x1, x2, x3 → alu_result = x2 + x3

dmem_rdata_mem:     32-bit
Data memory read ('den okunan veri (LOAD instruction'ları)
Örnek: LW x1, 0(x2) → dmem_rdata = memory[address] (4 bytes)

rd_mem:             5-bit
Hedef register numarası (WB stage'de yazlaacak register)
Örnek: ADD x7, x1, x2 → rd=7 (x7'ye yazılacak)
0 ise x0 (zero register, no write).

funct3_mem:         3-bit (YENİ - PROTOTIP-2 ÖNEMLİ)
Load byte width bilgisi:
- 3'b000: LB/SB (1 byte)
- 3'b001: LH/SH (2 bytes)
- 3'b010: LW/SW (4 bytes)
- 3'b100: LBU (unsigned byte)
- 3'b101: LHU (unsigned halfword)

WB stage'de funct3_wb kullanılarak:
- Kaç bite data valid?
- Sign-extend gerekli mi?
- Örnek: LBU x1, 0(x2) → funct3=3'b100, Memory byte okudu, zero-extend 32-bit yapılır

B. Kontrol Sinyalleri (MEM'den):

reg_write_en_mem:   1-bit
Register file yazma izni
- Arithmetic, LOAD: reg_write_en=1
- STORE, BRANCH: reg_write_en=0

pc_to_reg_mem:      1-bit
PC+4 register'a yazma (JAL/JALR için)
- JAL, JALR: pc_to_reg=1 (PC+4 yazılacak)
- Diğerleri: pc_to_reg=0

mem_to_reg_mem:     1-bit
Memory data register'a yazma (LOAD için)
- LOAD: mem_to_reg=1 (memory veri yazılacak)
- Arithmetic: mem_to_reg=0 (ALU sonucu yazılacak)

4. ÇIKIŞ SİNYALLERİ (WB aşamasına)

Çıkışlar giriş sinyalleriyle tamamen aynı, sadece "_wb" suffiksi:

pc_plus_4_wb:      32-bit
alu_result_wb:     32-bit
dmem_rdata_wb:     32-bit
rd_wb:             5-bit
funct3_wb:         3-bit
reg_write_en_wb:   1-bit
pc_to_reg_wb:      1-bit
mem_to_reg_wb:     1-bit

Bu sinyaller WB stage'deki bileşenlere gider:
- Write-back multiplexer (funct3_wb, mem_to_reg_wb, pc_to_reg_wb ile data seçim)
- Register File write port (rd_wb adresine, selected data yazılır)
- Forwarding Unit (mem_wb_rd, mem_wb_reg_write_en sinyalleri)

5. SEQUENTIAL LOGIC - EN BASIT DESIGN

always @(posedge clk or negedge rst_n) begin
    if (!rst_n) begin
        pc_plus_4_wb    <= {XLEN{1'b0}};
        alu_result_wb   <= {XLEN{1'b0}};
        dmem_rdata_wb   <= {XLEN{1'b0}};
        rd_wb           <= 5'b0;
        funct3_wb       <= 3'b0;
        reg_write_en_wb <= 1'b0;
        pc_to_reg_wb    <= 1'b0;
        mem_to_reg_wb   <= 1'b0;
    end else begin
        pc_plus_4_wb    <= pc_plus_4_mem;
        alu_result_wb   <= alu_result_mem;
        dmem_rdata_wb   <= dmem_rdata_mem;
        rd_wb           <= rd_mem;
        funct3_wb       <= funct3_mem;
        reg_write_en_wb <= reg_write_en_mem;
        pc_to_reg_wb    <= pc_to_reg_mem;
        mem_to_reg_wb   <= mem_to_reg_mem;
    end
end

ÖZEL NOKTA: flush, stall sinyalleri YOK!

Neden?
- WB son aşama, geriye pipeline hazard gitmez
- MEM/WB register'dan alınan data immediate register dosyasına yazılır
- Sonraki instruction'lar ID/EX ve forward wire'larından faydalanır
- O stage'lerde stall/flush zaten kontrol edilir

Basitlik: reset ve normal operasyon sadece.

E1. RESET (rst_n=0)

Sistem başlangıç:

pc_plus_4_wb    = 0
alu_result_wb   = 0
dmem_rdata_wb   = 0
rd_wb           = 0
funct3_wb       = 0
reg_write_en_wb = 0
pc_to_reg_wb    = 0
mem_to_reg_wb   = 0

WB aşamasında:
- rd_wb = 0 → x0 register (no actual write, since x0 hardwired)
- reg_write_en_wb = 0 → Write disabled (safe)
- Result: No side effect, pipeline clean

E2. NORMAL OPERATION (rst_n=1)

Every rising clock edge:

MEM stage output → MEM/WB register → WB stage

Example: ADD x7, x1, x2

Cycle 4 (MEM stage):
- alu_result_mem = x1 + x2 (computed Cycle 3)
- reg_write_en_mem = 1
- mem_to_reg_mem = 0
- pc_to_reg_mem = 0
- rd_mem = 7

Cycle 5 (WB stage):
- MEM/WB FF updates (posedge clk4→5)
- alu_result_wb = alu_result_mem (ALU result value)
- reg_write_en_wb = 1
- rd_wb = 7

WB stage logic:
- mem_to_reg_wb = 0 → Don't use dmem_rdata_wb
- pc_to_reg_wb = 0 → Don't use pc_plus_4_wb
- reg_file[7] = alu_result_wb ✓

Zaman geriye:

Cycle 5 start time:
- MEM/WB output stable (from Cycle 4 edge)
- WB stage: Combinational logic (multiplexer + sign-extension)
- Register file write: ~2 ns
- Data propagate complete Cycle 5 end

Cycle 6:
- Register x7 updated, next instruction read x7 can use forwarding (from mem_wb_rd, mem_wb_reg_write_en)

6. LOAD İNSTRUCTİON ÖRNEĞI (funct3_wb kritik)

Example: LH x3, 4(x5) (halfword, signed)

Cycle 1: IF fetch
Cycle 2: ID decode
Cycle 3: EX (address calc: x5 + 4)
Cycle 4: MEM (memory read)

Memory return:
- Address: 0x2004 (aligned to halfword)
- data: 16-bit = 0xFFAB (negative number)
- funct3 = 3'b001 (halfword)

Memory controller return 32-bit bus:
- dmem_rdata_mem = 0xFFFFFFAB (sign-extended internally? or raw 16-bit?)

Controversy: Typical memory interface return 32-bit:
- Option A: Memory controller sign/zero-extend (before MEM/WB)
- Option B: Raw 16-bit in lower bits, WB stage extend

Sizin design: funct3_wb taşınıyor, WB stage'de extend logical, so Option B.

Cycle 4 (MEM stage):
- Memory returns: 0x????FFAB (lower 16 bits valid, upper bits garbage or zero)
- funct3_mem = 3'b001 (halfword load)
- dmem_rdata_mem = 0x0000FFAB (or 0xGGGGFFAB, WB will fix)

Cycle 5 (WB stage):
- dmem_rdata_wb = 0x0000FFAB (or garbage)
- funct3_wb = 3'b001

WB sign-extend logic:

```verilog
// Sign extend data based on load type
always @(*) begin
    case (funct3_wb)
        3'b000: begin // LB (byte, signed)
            sign_bit = dmem_rdata_wb[7];
            writedata = {{24{sign_bit}}, dmem_rdata_wb[7:0]};
        end
        3'b001: begin // LH (halfword, signed)
            sign_bit = dmem_rdata_wb[15];
            writedata = {{16{sign_bit}}, dmem_rdata_wb[15:0]};
        end
        3'b010: begin // LW (word)
            writedata = dmem_rdata_wb;  // No extend needed
        end
        3'b100: begin // LBU (byte, unsigned)
            writedata = {{24{1'b0}}, dmem_rdata_wb[7:0]};
        end
        3'b101: begin // LHU (halfword, unsigned)
            writedata = {{16{1'b0}}, dmem_rdata_wb[15:0]};
        end
        default: writedata = 32'bx;
    endcase
end

// Register write
always @(*) begin
    if (reg_write_en_wb & (rd_wb != 5'b0)) begin
        regfile[rd_wb] <= (mem_to_reg_wb) ? writedata : alu_result_wb;
    end
end
```

funct3_wb sadece LOAD instruction'larda kullanılır. Arithmetic:
- funct3_wb = any value
- mem_to_reg_wb = 0 → writedata ignored, alu_result_wb used

7. YAZMA MULTİPLEKSER LOGİĞİ (WB stage'de)

Register File write data seçimi:

```verilog
wire [XLEN-1:0] write_value;

always @(*) begin
    if (pc_to_reg_wb) begin
        write_value = pc_plus_4_wb;  // JAL/JALR
    end else if (mem_to_reg_wb) begin
        write_value = extended_dmem_rdata;  // LOAD (with sign-extension applied)
    end else begin
        write_value = alu_result_wb;  // Arithmetic operations
    end
end

// Register File
always @(posedge clk) begin
    if (reg_write_en_wb & (rd_wb != 5'b0)) begin
        regfile[rd_wb] <= write_value;
    end
end
```

Priority: pc_to_reg > mem_to_reg > alu_result

JAL x1, target:
- pc_to_reg_wb = 1
- write_value = pc_plus_4_wb = return_address
- x1 = return_address ✓

LW x3, 0(x5):
- pc_to_reg_wb = 0
- mem_to_reg_wb = 1
- write_value = extended_dmem_rdata
- x3 = memory_data (sign-extended) ✓

ADD x3, x1, x2:
- pc_to_reg_wb = 0
- mem_to_reg_wb = 0
- write_value = alu_result_wb
- x3 = x1 + x2 ✓

8. TIMING ANALIZ - WB STAGE

WB stage critical path:

Path 1 (Register file write):
- MEM/WB FF: tco = 3 ns (FF output stable)
- Sign-extend logic: 1 ns (mux + bit select)
- Register file write decoder: 2 ns (address to write line)
- FF setup: 2 ns (register input setup)
- Total: 8 ns

Path 2 (Forwarding unit):
- MEM/WB FF: tco = 3 ns
- Comparator (mem_wb_rd == id_ex_rs1): 2 ns
- MUX selector output: 1 ns
- Total: 6 ns (fast)

Timing closure @ 100 MHz:
- Path 1: 8 ns < 10 ns OK
- Path 2: 6 ns < 10 ns OK

WB stage: No timing critical (simplest stage).

9. PROTOTIP-1 vs PROTOTIP-2

Sizin comment:

// Degisiklik (Prototip-1'den farklar):
//   - funct3_mem -> funct3_wb eklendi (load sign-extension icin)
//   - WB asamasinda funct3 ile LB/LBU/LH/LHU/LW ayirt edilecek

Prototip-1: funct3_wb YOK (eksik tasarım!)

Problem Prototip-1:
```verilog
// Prototip-1 (eksik)
module mem_wb_reg (
    input wire dmem_rdata_mem,
    output reg dmem_rdata_wb,
    // NO funct3!
);
```

WB stage'de:
- LH instruction sonrası dmem_rdata_wb = 0x0000FFAB
- How to sign-extend?
- funct3 bilinmiyor! (Was it LH signed or LHU unsigned?)
- Result: Wrong sign-extension!

Prototip-2 (Sizin - Doğru):
```verilog
module mem_wb_reg (
    input wire [2:0] funct3_mem,
    output reg [2:0] funct3_wb,
    // YES funct3!
);
```

WB stage'de:
- funct3_wb = 3'b001 (LH)
- Know it's signed halfword
- Apply sign-extension: 0x0000FFAB → 0xFFFFFFAB ✓

Prototip-2 design fix: CRITICAL CORRECTION!

11. REGISTER FILE YAZMA DETAYLARı

Register file 32x32-bit (32 registers, 32-bit each):

Read ports (Combinational):
- regfile[rs1] → rs1_data (ID stage)
- regfile[rs2] → rs2_data (ID stage)

Write port (Sequential):
- Rising clock edge: regfile[rd_wb] <= write_value (WB stage)

x0 special:
- Read x0: always returns 0x0
- Write x0: ignored (no effect)

Logically:
```verilog
always @(posedge clk) begin
    if (reg_write_en_wb & (rd_wb != 5'b0)) begin
        regs[rd_wb] <= write_value;
    end
end

assign rs1_data = (rs1 == 5'b0) ? 32'b0 : regs[rs1];
assign rs2_data = (rs2 == 5'b0) ? 32'b0 : regs[rs2];
```

Prototip-2: rd_wb = 0 check built-in sizin tasarımda (implicit assume WB stage checks).

12. MULTI-CYCLE WRITE BACK

Vectorized example:

Instruction sequence:
1. ADD x1, x2, x3        → Cycle 5 WB (write x1)
2. AND x4, x5, x6        → Cycle 5 WB (write x4)

Wait! Both same cycle?

In standard 5-stage pipeline: One instruction WB per cycle (sequential).

Sequence (corrected):

Instruction 1: ADD x1, ...
- Cycle 1: IF
- Cycle 2: ID
- Cycle 3: EX
- Cycle 4: MEM
- Cycle 5: WB (x1 = result)

Instruction 2: AND x4, ...
- Cycle 1: IF
- Cycle 2: ID
- Cycle 3: EX
- Cycle 4: MEM
- Cycle 5: WB (x4 = result)

No collision: Cycle 5'de Inst1 WB, Cycle 6'de Inst2 WB.

Pipeline fill: 5 instructions in flight. Each WB in separate cycle.

Throughput: 1 instruction WB per cycle (100% stable).

13. EXCEPTION VE INTERRUPT - NOT SUPPORTED

Sizin MEM/WB register: No exception field!

Modern CPU:
```verilog
input wire [31:0] exception_code_mem;
output reg [31:0] exception_code_wb;
```

If exception detected (e.g., illegal instruction, page fault):
- exception_code_wb = exception_type
- WB stage: Don't commit, instead:
  - Jump PC to exception_handler_address
  - Save return PC to exception_pc register
  - Set exception status bits

Sizin design: No exception (RV32I, basic, no traps).

14. VIRTUAL MEMORY VE TLB - NOT SUPPORTED

Address translation:

Modern CPU memory hierarchy:
- Virtual address (VA) from instruction
- TLB (Translation Lookaside Buffer) lookup
- Physical address (PA)
- Memory access

Sizin tasarım: Direct physical address (no virtual memory).

If VM support needed:
```verilog
input wire [XLEN-1:0] virt_addr_mem;
output reg [XLEN-1:0] virt_addr_wb;
```

Virt to phys mapping in WB stage (or earlier).

15. SİZİN TASARIMIN GÜÇLÜ YÖNLERI

1. Simple, clean Verilog
2. Correct reset behavior
3. funct3 support (sign-extension critical)
4. All three write paths (pc_to_reg, mem_to_reg, alu_result)
5. Parametrik XLEN
6. Asynchronous reset
7. Non-blocking assignment (safe)

16. EKSİKLİKLER VE İYİLEŞTİRMELER

Eksiklik 1: Exception handling yok
- exception_code field needed
- Can add exception types (illegal inst, page fault, divide-by-zero)

Eksiklik 2: Virtual memory yok
- virt_addr field needed
- TLB integration

Eksiklik 3: FPU write-back support yok
- Floating point data format
- IEEE 754 handling

Eksiklik 4: Cache coherency yok (multi-core)
- Atomic operation tracking
- Load-link/store-conditional state

17. FORWARDING UNIT INTEGRATION

Forwarding unit reads MEM/WB stage:

```verilog
// In CPU top-level
forwarding_unit fwd (
    .mem_wb_rd(rd_wb),
    .mem_wb_reg_write_en(reg_write_en_wb),
    .mem_wb_data(mem_wb_alu_result),  // or dmem_rdata (needs mux)
    ...
);
```

mem_wb_rd, mem_wb_reg_write_en, mem_wb_alu_result sinyalleri next cycle'da gelen rs1, rs2 control register'lara karşı check edilir.

CRITICAL: mem_wb_alu_result seçimi:
- If mem_to_reg=1: dmem_rdata_wb (memory data)
- If pc_to_reg=1: pc_plus_4_wb (return address)
- Else: alu_result_wb

Ama forwarding unit simple olduğu için: Just forward alu_result_wb?

Design consideration:
```verilog
// Option 1: Simple (current)
assign forward_value = alu_result_wb;  // What if mem_to_reg_wb=1?

// Option 2: Correct
always @(*) begin
    if (mem_to_reg_wb) forward_value = dmem_rdata_wb;
    else if (pc_to_reg_wb) forward_value = pc_plus_4_wb;
    else forward_value = alu_result_wb;
end
```

Sizin tasarımda: Forwarding unit ayrı hardware, bu decision WB stage multiplexer'da yapılır.

18. POWER MANAGEMENT

MEM/WB register disipation:

- 8 registers * 32-bit = 256 bits
- FF toggle frequency ≈ 50% (CMOS dynamic)
- Power = 0.5 * Cv^2 * f * #transitions
- Estimate: ~2-3 mW @ 100 MHz (small contributor)

Total pipeline:
- IF/ID: ~2 mW
- ID/EX: ~5 mW
- EX/MEM: ~5 mW
- MEM/WB: ~2 mW
- Total: ~14 mW registers

CPU total: ~50-100 mW @ 100 MHz (including memories, logic).

19. SONUÇ

Sizin mem_wb_reg.v tasarımı:

Güçlü Tarafları:
- Simple, clean design
- Three write paths (arithmetic, load, jump)
- funct3 support for sign-extension (Prototip-2 FIX!)
- Correct register file write
- Parametrik XLEN
- Asynchronous reset
- No timing critical (easiest stage)

Eksikleri:
- Exception handling yok
- Virtual memory yok
- FPU support yok
- Cache coherency yok

RV32I 5-stage in-order pipeline için PERFECT!

Register count: 8 * 32-bit = 256 flip-flops
Transistor: ~460 (CMOS)
Power: ~2-3 mW @ 100 MHz
Timing: Non-critical (~8 ns path, 10 ns clock margin good)

Pipeline WB stage: Final commitment to architecture state (register repository)

Key feature (Prototip-2 improvement): funct3_wb for correct load sign-extension!

================================================================================
PC.V ANALİZİ (Program Counter Module)
=============

1. YAPININ GENEL ÖZETİ

Sizin PC (Program Counter) modülü, CPU'nun instruction adresini saklamak ve güncellemekten sorumlu en temel bileşendir.

Temel işlev:
- Mevcut instruction'ın memory address'ini sakla (pc_current)
- Her clock rising edge'de pc_next değeri ile güncelle
- System reset'te 0x00000000'dan başla
- IF (Instruction Fetch) stage'e instruction memory address'i sağla

Veri akışı: pc_next → PC register → Instruction Memory address → IF stage

Kritik nokta: PC register single point of state progression. pc_next selection (normal/branch/jump) kritik!

2. MODULE PARAMETRESI

parameter XLEN = 32

Program counter width: 32-bit (4 GB address space)
RV64 için XLEN=64 (16 EB address space possible, but typically 48-bit VA)
RV32E (embedded) için XLEN=32 (fixed)

Sizin tasarım XLEN parametrik → flexible architecture.

3. GİRİŞ SİNYALLERİ

clk:        1-bit
System clock, rising edge triggered
PC update synchronized with CPU pipeline.

rst_n:      1-bit
Asynchronous active-low reset
On power-up or reset button: rst_n=0 → PC=0x0

pc_next:    32-bit (veya XLEN-bit)
Bir sonraki instruction adres'i
Kaynak: IF stage'den pc_next = (branch_taken) ? branch_target : (pc + 4)

Bu input'un kaynağı IF/ID stage logic'dedir:

Sequential paths:
1. Normal: pc_next = pc_current + 4
2. Branch: pc_next = pc_current + branch_offset
3. JALR: pc_next = alu_result (register'dan gelen indirect jump)

4. ÇIKIŞ SİNYALLERİ

pc_current:    32-bit
Mevcut instruction adres'i (output)
Instruction memory'ye address bus olarak bağlanır.
Ayrıca pc_plus_4 calculation'ında kullanılır (pc + 4 = branch return address).

Usage:
- Instruction fetch stage: instr_mem[pc_current]
- Branch offset calculation: target = pc_current + offset
- JAL/JALR: x1 = pc_current + 4 (return address)

5. SEQUENTIAL LOGIC - BASIT DESIGN

always @(posedge clk or negedge rst_n) begin
    if (!rst_n) begin
        pc_current <= {XLEN{1'b0}};  // Reset: all zeros
    end else begin
        pc_current <= pc_next;        // Normal: update each cycle
    end
end

Kontrol: 2-to-1 implicit MUX:
- If rst_n=0: pc_current = 0
- Else: pc_current = pc_next

State transition:
- POWER_UP (rst_n=0): PC initialization
- RUNNING (rst_n=1): Sequential update

E1. POWER-UP / RESET SEQUENCE

Time t=0: System power on, rst_n=0

PC register immediately reset (asynchronous):
pc_current = 0x00000000

Instruction memory:
- Address bus = 0x00000000
- Output = instruction at address 0 (bootloader start)

Timing:
- rst_n falls (negative edge)
- PC FF asynchronously clears (tsu, th not applicable)
- Instruction memory address valid immediately (combinational)
- IF stage sees first instruction

Time t=1: First clock rising edge (rst_n already 1)

pc_next value (from pc_next input):
- During reset phase, pc_next = undefined (don't care)
- Or, pc_next can be pre-driven = 4 (next address)

At rising edge:
- IF condition: if (!rst_n) takes priority
- PC stays 0

E2. NORMAL OPERATION

Time t=2 onwards: rst_n=1, normal pipeline

Each clock cycle:

Cycle N (falling):
- IF stage: Fetches instruction from pc_current (still previous value)
- ID stage: Decode
- EX stage: ALU, branch condition calculate
- etc.

Cycle N rising edge:
- pc_next = (branch_taken) ? branch_target : (pc + 4)
- pc_current <= pc_next (FF update)

Cycle N+1:
- IF fetches from new pc_current

Timeline example (no branch):

Cycle 1:
- pc_current = 0x0000
- IF: fetch instr[0x0000] = "ADDI x1, x0, 5"
- pc_next input = 0x0004

Cycle 2 (rising edge):
- pc_current <= 0x0004
- PC register updated

- IF: fetch instr[0x0004] = "ADD x2, x1, x0"
- pc_next input = 0x0008

Cycle 3:
- pc_current = 0x0008
- IF: fetch instr[0x0008] = next

Result: Sequential instruction fetch, 1 instruction per cycle (if no stalls).

E3. BRANCH SCENARIO

Instruction: BEQ x1, x2, -8 (backward branch, offset = -8 means PC-8)

Cycle 1:
- pc_current = 0x0010
- IF: fetch instr[0x0010] = BEQ

Cycle 2:
- ID: decode BEQ
- pc_current = 0x0014 (pc+4)

Cycle 3:
- EX: ALU computes x1-x2, branch_condition = (x1==x2)
- If true: pc_next = 0x0010 + (-8) = 0x0008
- If false: pc_next = 0x0014 + 4 = 0x0018 (default from NOT branch)

Wait! How does pc_next know when to branch?

PC NEXT LOGIC (in IF stage, combinational):

```verilog
wire [XLEN-1:0] pc_plus_4 = pc_current + 4;
wire [XLEN-1:0] pc_branch_target = pc_current + {{20{imm[11]}}, imm[11:0]};

// MUX: Select next PC
assign pc_next = (branch_taken_ex && branch_valid_ex) ? pc_branch_target : pc_plus_4;
```

branch_taken_ex: from EX stage (branch resolved)
branch_valid_ex: from control unit (this instruction is branch)

BUT: Complex feedback!

EX stage branch condition (Cycle 3):
- Calculated from ALU (previous EX instruction finish)
- branch_condition output
- Must affect NEXT Cycle's pc_next

Timing:
Cycle N EX:
- branch_condition calc
- branch_target = pc_ex + offset

Cycle N+1 IF:
- pc_next uses branch_target (from previous cycle)
- PC update

This multi-cycle latency explain branch penalty!

6. PC+4 CALCULATION

WHERE is pc+4 calculated?

Two locations:

Location A: IF stage (for next sequential PC)
```verilog
assign pc_plus_4_if = pc_current + 4;
```
Used as next sequential address.

Location B: ID/EX/MEM/WB pipeline
```verilog
wire [XLEN-1:0] pc_plus_4_id = pc_plus_4_if;  // Pass through
```
Used for JAL/JALR return address (x1 = PC+4).

Both derived from same source (pc_current), guaranteed consistent.

7. WORD-ALIGNED ACCESS

RISC-V instruction: 32-bit (4 bytes)

Memory address increment: pc_next = pc_current + 4 (word-aligned)

Example:
pc_current = 0x00001000 (aligned to 4-byte boundary)
pc_next = 0x00001004
pc+4 = 0x00001008

Assumption: instructions always 4-byte aligned (RISC-V standard).

If misaligned branch:
- target = 0x00001001 (not aligned!)
- Instruction fetch: Error (undefined behavior)
- Some CPU's: Exception (instruction misaligned)

Sizin design: No alignment check (assumes correct code).

If safety needed:
```verilog
assign pc_next_aligned = {pc_next[XLEN-1:2], 2'b00};  // Force 4-byte align
```

8. TIMING ANALIZ - PC REGISTER

PC FF critical path:

Path 1 (PC update):
- Combinational logic (IF stage): pc_next calc = 3 ns (adder)
- PC FF: tsu = 2 ns (setup time before clock)
- Total: 5 ns

Path 2 (PC output to memory):
- PC FF: tco = 3 ns (clock-to-output)
- Instruction memory: taa = 4 ns (address to data)
- IF stage decode: 2 ns
- Total: 9 ns

Clock period @ 100 MHz = 10 ns.
Slack = 10 - 9 = 1 ns (TIGHT!)

POTENTIAL OPTIMIZATION: Register pc+4 separately

```verilog
// PC+4 register (pre-calculated)
always @(posedge clk or negedge rst_n) begin
    if (!rst_n) pc_plus_4_current <= 4;
    else pc_plus_4_current <= pc_current + 4;
end
```

Benefit: Path 2 becomes 3 ns (FF to memo, no adder).

9. HAZARD DETECTION VE PC KONTROL

Hazard Detection Unit control'ü:

```verilog
// In top-level CPU
hazard_detection_unit hdu (
    ...
    .stall(stall),
    .pc_write_en(pc_write_en),
    .if_id_write_en(if_id_write_en),
    ...
);

// NOT in PC module itself!
// Instead, in IF stage:
wire [XLEN-1:0] pc_next = (pc_write_en) ? pc_input : pc_current;
```

Wait! Sizin PC module does NOT have pc_write_en input!

This is design choice:

Option A (Sizin design):
- PC module: Always update (pc_current <= pc_next every cycle)
- pc_next input controlled elsewhere
- Hazard Detection: Keeps pc_next frozen (not pc_current)

Option B (Alternative):
- PC module: Has pc_write_en input
- PC module: pc_current <= (pc_write_en) ? pc_next : pc_current
- Hazard Detection: Sets pc_write_en=0 to freeze

Sizin design (Option A): Cleaner separation. pc_next logic outside PC module.

10. BRANCH FLUSH COORDINATION

Branch misprediction flow:

Cycle N (EX stage):
- Branch resolved (condition calculated)
- flush_signal = 1 (mispredicted)

Cycle N+1:
- flush signal to IF/ID register (insert NOP)
- PC update continues normal
- pc_next input already from new target

Result: IF/ID flushed (NOP), PC updated (branch taken).

11. STALL HANDLING

Load-use hazard stall:

Cycle N (EX stage):
- Load instruction, hazard detected
- stall = 1

Cycle N+1:
- PC update: pc_next = (stall) ? pc_current : pc_next_new

Wait! This NOT in PC module!

Let me reconsider:

Actually, stall control is:

```verilog
// In IF stage (above PC module)
wire [XLEN-1:0] pc_next_combined;

always @(*) begin
    if (stall) pc_next_combined = pc_current;  // Hold PC
    else pc_next_combined = pc_calc;  // Normal
end

// Then PC module
pc pc_reg (
    .pc_next(pc_next_combined),
    ...
);
```

Stall logic BEFORE PC module input.

Effect:
- stall=1: pc_next = pc_current (feedback loop)
- PC register: pc_current <= pc_current (no change)
- Pipeline frozen

This is clever! Stall handled at input level.

12. GITHUB POPÜLERİTESİ TASARIMLARIYLA KARŞILAŞTIRMA

PicoRV32 (Clifford Wolf)
GitHub: https://github.com/cliffordwolf/picorv32
Stars: 4.1k

PicoRV32 PC:
- Same simple design
- Single FF register
- pc_next input controlled by mux
- ~30 lines

Sizinle Fark: Identical approach.

VexRISCV (SpinalHDL)
GitHub: https://github.com/SpinalHDL/VexRISCV
Stars: 1.4k

VexRISCV PC:
- Parametric width
- Branch prediction support (next PC cached)
- pc_write_en control
- Scala HDL

Sizinle Fark: VexRISCV has pc_write_en (Option B), prediction buffer.

Rocket Chip (UC Berkeley)
GitHub: https://github.com/chipsalliance/rocket-chip
Stars: 1.8k

Rocket Chip PC:
- 64-bit address support
- Prefetch buffer
- Branch prediction (BTB)
- Multiple PC tracks (speculative)

CVA6 (Thales)
GitHub: https://github.com/openhwgroup/cva6
Stars: 1.2k

CVA6 PC:
- Out-of-order execution (multiple PCs in flight)
- Instruction fetch queue
- TLB integration
- Exception PC save

BOOM (OOO CPU)
GitHub: https://github.com/riscv-boom/riscv-boom
Stars: 0.8k
